## Best date plans

### 2026-11-14 — Roebling Rink opening-day free skating sessions + Casa Oaxaca

**Roebling Rink opening-day free skating sessions, then a meal.** Mexican at Casa Oaxaca. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10:30 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Emily+Warren+Roebling+Plaza%2C+Brooklyn+Bridge+Park%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=301+South+End+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.roeblingrink.com/) · [Restaurant](https://www.casaoaxacany.com/)

#### [Roebling Rink opening-day free skating sessions](https://www.roeblingrink.com/)

10:30 AM · sports · Free session admission; possible skate-rental charge · Roebling Rink, Emily Warren Roebling Plaza · DUMBO · Brooklyn · Score 69.0  
The waterfront rink opens its season with free admission for its first two skating sessions beneath the Brooklyn Bridge.
**Timing:** Two separate sessions: 10:30am–noon and 12:30–2pm; not continuous skating.
**Practical note:** Free-ticket inventory is limited to these sessions. Skate rental is not confirmed as included. Saturday is forecast to be chilly and breezy.
**Why now:** Opening day of the November 14–January 31 rink season.
**On-sale watch:** Select a free opening-day session; availability is not guaranteed.
[Official](https://www.roeblingrink.com/) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.roeblingrink.com/) · [Discovered via [local] The Skint](https://www.theskint.com/ongoing-events/)

#### [MoMA free Friday evening](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 46.0  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.

## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 14, 2026: recent openings, everyday values, a November 16 wine takeover and Thanksgiving plans. Weekday offers are not available this Saturday; holiday availability and remaining reservations are not guaranteed.

Dining research: 2026-11-14T10:47:13+00:00

No Michelin awards are added. GUI's captured website describes it as Michelin Selected, and KRU's describes inclusion in the Michelin Guide; neither statement establishes a star. The restaurant-linked Michelin page returned no readable content: https://guide.michelin.com/us/en/new-york-state/new-york/restaurant/gui-1207502. Current Michelin recognition was not independently verified.

Reservation availability, required deposits, whole-table participation, supplements and tax/tip/service charges remain unverified. Holiday closures unconfirmed. This is an existing tasting menu, not a dated event or claimed discount.  
Published: 2026-11-08  
[Source](https://www.theinfatuation.com/new-york/guides/most-affordable-tasting-menu-nyc)

### Dirt Candy's seasonal vegetarian tasting: reported at $110

Special menus — Dirt Candy

A higher-budget set-menu option with five seasonal courses and additional small bites; a vegan version is available.

Price: $110 tasting menu; optional wine pairing $55, both reported November 8. Official FAQ confirms no tipping.  
Schedule: Tuesday–Saturday 5:30–10:30pm.  
Conditions: Reservation requires a credit card. Official cancellation policy charges $50 per person unless at least 24 hours' notice is given. Gluten-free dinner cannot be accommodated; other allergies require advance notice and are not guaranteed accommodation. Tax treatment and current booking availability unknown. Existing seasonal tasting menu, not a newly launched event.  
Published: 2026-11-08

#### [Elektra — remaining dates amid documented sellouts](https://tfana.org/events/elektra-2026-11-14-730-pm)

2026-11-25 · 7:30 PM · theater · Regular seats $77–$105 including handling; premium seats cost more. · Polonsky Shakespeare Center · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 69.0  
Daniel Fish directs Sophokles' tragedy in Anne Carson's translation, with choral music by Ted Hearne. Elektra and Orestes confront the consequences of revenge.
**Timing:** November 25 performance at 7:30pm; the run ends December 6 at 7:30pm.
**Practical note:** No late seating. November 25 has a public seat-selection link without a sold-out label; this is not a guarantee of inventory. Regular tickets are $70–$98 plus a $7 handling fee.
**Why now:** Several performances beyond the seven-day window are already sold out or severely limited; the engagement ends December 6.
**Hot booking alert:** The official calendar marks November 24 and December 1–3 sold out, November 21–22 matinees and December 4–5 limited to no availability, and the final December 6 evening limited to no availability.
